## Description

Microsoft Build 2026 Day 2 is live! Start your morning with GitHub, VS Code, and everything Copilot, then dive into a full day of live programming and breakouts.

🎯 What's happening:
• GitHub Copilot deep dives
• VS Code announcements and demos
• Live coding with Microsoft and GitHub engineers
• Foundry sessions
• Community speakers and creators
• Select breakout sessions streamed live

🕘 Starts 9:00 AM PT

Full schedule...
9:00 Day 1 Recap: What You Missed (or Watched Twice) — Pierce Boggan, Cassidy Williams, Burke Holland
9:10 Agent Skill — Addy Osmani

10:55 Working with Models

12:15 Toolboxes in Foundry — Linda Li, Atul Aggarwal, Seth Juarez
12:30 From Issue to Merge in One Loop: The GitHub Copilot App — Seth Juarez, Burke Holland
